     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""
     3	Projected Maxwell primitive bridge
     4	
     5	This script starts one step closer to the Stage-4 / Stage-5 one-port moving-throat
     6	bundle. Instead of inserting abstract bundle corrections z_n and n_n directly,
     7	it perturbs the primitive Maxwell/mixed one-port data:
     8	
     9	    Q, S2, H, Delta, P, Gw
    10	
    11	by small mouth-local projected-Maxwell slippages:
    12	
    13	    q1, s1, h1, d1, p1, g1
    14	
    15	and derives the induced bundle corrections
    16	
    17	    z0, z2, z4, n0, n2, n4
    18	
    19	exactly at first order.
    20	
    21	These are the quantities that then feed the full-bundle formulas for
    22	D0, D2, D4, P0, P2, P4, Xi1, and the isotropic compatibility surface.
    23	"""
    24	
    25	from __future__ import annotations
    26	
    27	import sympy as sp
    28	
    29	
    30	def banner(title: str) -> str:
    31	    return "\n" + "=" * 96 + f"\n{title}\n" + "=" * 96 + "\n"
    32	
    33	
    34	def line(label: str, expr: sp.Expr | str) -> str:
    35	    return f"{label}\n  {expr}\n"
    36	
    37	
    38	def assert_zero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
    39	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    40	    if residue != 0:
    41	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} failed: {sp.sstr(residue)}")
    42	
    43	
    44	def assert_nonzero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
    45	    value = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    46	    if value == 0:
    47	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} unexpectedly vanished")
    48	
    49	
    50	def main() -> None:
    51	    Q, S2, H, Delta, P, Gw = sp.symbols("Q S2 H Delta P Gw", nonzero=True)
    52	    q1, s1, h1, d1, p1, g1 = sp.symbols("q1 s1 h1 d1 p1 g1")
    53	    ell = sp.symbols("ell")
    54	
    55	    # Stage-4 / Stage-5 primitive one-port formulas
    56	    Z0 = Q / Delta
    57	    Z2 = (Q * S2 - H * Delta) / Delta**2
    58	    Z4 = (Q * (S2**2 - Delta) - S2 * H * Delta) / Delta**3
    59	
    60	    N0 = P**2 / Delta**2
    61	    N2 = 2 * P * (P * S2 - Delta * Gw) / Delta**3
    62	    N4 = (Delta**2 * Gw**2 - 2 * Delta * P**2 - 4 * Delta * P * S2 * Gw + 3 * P**2 * S2**2) / Delta**4
    63	
    64	    subs = {
    65	        Q: Q + ell * q1,
    66	        S2: S2 + ell * s1,
    67	        H: H + ell * h1,
    68	        Delta: Delta + ell * d1,
    69	        P: P + ell * p1,
    70	        Gw: Gw + ell * g1,
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    def dlin(expr: sp.Expr) -> sp.Expr:
    74	        return sp.simplify((sp.series(sp.expand(expr.subs(subs)), ell, 0, 2).removeO() - expr) / ell)
    75	
    76	    z0 = dlin(Z0)
    77	    z2 = dlin(Z2)
    78	    z4 = dlin(Z4)
    79	    n0 = dlin(N0)
    80	    n2 = dlin(N2)
    81	    n4 = dlin(N4)
    82	
    83	    assert_zero("z0 closed form", z0 - (Delta * q1 - Q * d1) / Delta**2)
    84	    assert_zero(
    85	        "z2 closed form",
    86	        z2 - (-Delta**2 * h1 + Delta * (H * d1 + Q * s1 + S2 * q1) - 2 * Q * S2 * d1) / Delta**3,
    87	    )
    88	    assert_zero(
    89	        "z4 closed form",
    90	        z4
    91	        - (
    92	            -Delta**2 * H * s1
    93	            - Delta**2 * S2 * h1
    94	            - Delta**2 * q1
    95	            + 2 * Delta * H * S2 * d1
    96	            + 2 * Delta * Q * S2 * s1
    97	            + 2 * Delta * Q * d1
    98	            + Delta * S2**2 * q1
    99	            - 3 * Q * S2**2 * d1
   100	        )
   101	        / Delta**4,
   102	    )
   103	    assert_zero("n0 closed form", n0 - 2 * P * (Delta * p1 - P * d1) / Delta**3)
   104	    assert_zero(
   105	        "n2 closed form",
   106	        n2
   107	        - (
   108	            -(
   109	                2 * Delta**2 * (Gw * p1 + P * g1)
   110	                - 2 * Delta * P * (2 * Gw * d1 + P * s1 + 2 * S2 * p1)
   111	                + 6 * P**2 * S2 * d1
   112	            )
   113	            / Delta**4
   114	        ),
   115	    )
   116	    assert_zero(
   117	        "n4 closed form",
   118	        n4
   119	        - 2
   120	        * (
   121	            Delta**3 * Gw * g1
   122	            - Delta**2 * Gw**2 * d1
   123	            - 2 * Delta**2 * Gw * P * s1
   124	            - 2 * Delta**2 * Gw * S2 * p1
   125	            - 2 * Delta**2 * P * S2 * g1
   126	            - 2 * Delta**2 * P * p1
   127	            + 6 * Delta * Gw * P * S2 * d1
   128	            + 3 * Delta * P**2 * S2 * s1
   129	            + 3 * Delta * P**2 * d1
   130	            + 3 * Delta * P * S2**2 * p1
   131	            - 6 * P**2 * S2**2 * d1
   132	        )
   133	        / Delta**5,
   134	    )
   135	
   136	    slips = {Q: q1, S2: s1, H: h1, Delta: d1, P: p1, Gw: g1}
   137	
   138	    def frechet(expr: sp.Expr) -> sp.Expr:
   139	        return sp.simplify(sum(sp.diff(expr, var) * slip for var, slip in slips.items()))
   140	
   141	    assert_zero("z0 Frechet derivative", z0 - frechet(Z0))
   142	    assert_zero("z2 Frechet derivative", z2 - frechet(Z2))
   143	    assert_zero("z4 Frechet derivative", z4 - frechet(Z4))
   144	    assert_zero("n0 Frechet derivative", n0 - frechet(N0))
   145	    assert_zero("n2 Frechet derivative", n2 - frechet(N2))
   146	    assert_zero("n4 Frechet derivative", n4 - frechet(N4))
   147	
   148	    D0sym, N0sym = sp.symbols("D0 N0", nonzero=True)
   149	    Xi1_static = sp.simplify(n0 / N0 + z0 / D0sym)
   150	    assert_zero(
   151	        "primitive Xi static formula",
   152	        Xi1_static - (2*p1/P - 2*d1/Delta + (Delta*q1 - Q*d1)/(D0sym*Delta**2)),
   153	    )
   154	
   155	    K, B0, Z0slot, N0base, D0target, Ptarget, S, T = sp.symbols("K B0 Z0slot N0base D0target Ptarget S T", nonzero=True)
   156	    K_one_p = sp.solve(
   157	        sp.Eq((K - B0 - (Z0slot + ell * z0)) * (T + ell * z4), 3 * (S + ell * z2) ** 2),
   158	        K,
   159	    )[0]
   160	    K_norm_p = sp.solve(
   161	        sp.Eq((N0base + ell * n0) / (K - B0 - (Z0slot + ell * z0)), Ptarget),
   162	        K,
   163	    )[0]
   164	    compat_direct_p = sp.simplify((N0base + ell * n0) / Ptarget - 3 * (S + ell * z2) ** 2 / (T + ell * z4))
   165	    Ptarget_transport_p = sp.simplify((N0base + ell * n0) / D0target)
   166	    K_norm_transport_p = sp.solve(
   167	        sp.Eq((N0base + ell * n0) / (K - B0 - (Z0slot + ell * z0)), Ptarget_transport_p),
   168	        K,
   169	    )[0]
   170	    compat_transport_p = sp.simplify(K_norm_transport_p - K_one_p)
   171	    compat_direct = compat_direct_p.subs(ell, 0)
   172	    compat_transport = compat_transport_p.subs(ell, 0)
   173	    compat = K_norm_p.subs(ell, 0) - K_one_p.subs(ell, 0)
   174	    dCompat = sp.simplify((sp.series(sp.expand(K_norm_p - K_one_p), ell, 0, 2).removeO() - compat) / ell)
   175	    dCompat_direct = sp.simplify((sp.series(sp.expand(compat_direct_p), ell, 0, 2).removeO() - compat_direct) / ell)
   176	    dCompat_transport = sp.simplify((sp.series(sp.expand(compat_transport_p), ell, 0, 2).removeO() - compat_transport) / ell)
   177	    assert_zero(
   178	        "K_one solve round-trip",
   179	        (K_one_p - B0 - (Z0slot + ell * z0)) * (T + ell * z4) - 3 * (S + ell * z2) ** 2,
   180	    )
   181	    assert_zero(
   182	        "K_norm solve round-trip",
   183	        (N0base + ell * n0) / (K_norm_p - B0 - (Z0slot + ell * z0)) - Ptarget,
   184	    )
   185	    assert_zero(
   186	        "primitive compatibility shift from competing K surfaces",
   187	        dCompat_direct - (n0 / Ptarget - 6 * S * z2 / T + 3 * S**2 * z4 / T**2),
   188	    )
   189	    assert_zero("primitive transported-target normalization K surface", K_norm_transport_p - (B0 + Z0slot + ell * z0 + D0target))
   190	    assert_zero(
   191	        "primitive transported-target compatibility surface",
   192	        compat_transport_p - (D0target - 3 * (S + ell * z2) ** 2 / (T + ell * z4)),
   193	    )
   194	    assert_zero(
   195	        "primitive transported-target compatibility shift",
   196	        dCompat_transport - (-6 * S * z2 / T + 3 * S**2 * z4 / T**2),
   197	    )
   198	    # The derived z0 = (Delta*q1 - Q*d1)/Delta**2 enters K_norm_p and K_one_p
   199	    # through identical +ell*z0 terms, so it must cancel from their difference.
   200	    # The substantive check is that the q1 and d1 partials of the compatibility
   201	    # difference receive NO contribution from the z0 channel: they must equal
   202	    # the q1 and d1 partials of compat_direct_p, which depends only on n0, z2, z4.
   203	    for slip in (q1, d1):
   204	        assert_zero(
   205	            f"primitive fixed-target compatibility has no z0 channel in {slip}",
   206	            sp.diff(K_norm_p - K_one_p, slip) - sp.diff(compat_direct_p, slip),
   207	        )
   208	        assert_zero(
   209	            f"primitive transported-target compatibility has no z0 channel in {slip}",
   210	            sp.diff(K_norm_transport_p - K_one_p, slip) - sp.diff(compat_transport_p, slip),
   211	        )
   212	    # Conversely, the normalization K surface alone DOES retain a q1/d1 channel
   213	    # through the derived z0.
   214	    assert_nonzero(
   215	        "primitive normalization K surface retains q1 channel from z0",
   216	        sp.diff(K_norm_p, q1),
   217	    )
   218	    assert_nonzero(
   219	        "primitive normalization K surface retains d1 channel from z0",
   220	        sp.diff(K_norm_p, d1),
   221	    )
   222	    assert_nonzero(
   223	        "mutated primitive compatibility transport should fail",
   224	        dCompat_direct - (n0 / Ptarget - 6 * S * z2 / T - 3 * S**2 * z4 / T**2),
   225	    )
   226	    assert_nonzero(
   227	        "mutated primitive transported-target compatibility should fail",
   228	        dCompat_transport - (-6 * S * z2 / T - 3 * S**2 * z4 / T**2),
   229	    )
   230	
   231	    lines = []
   232	    lines.append(banner("1) Primitive one-port Maxwell/mixed formulas"))
   233	    lines.append(line("Z0 =", Z0))
   234	    lines.append(line("Z2 =", Z2))
   235	    lines.append(line("Z4 =", Z4))
   236	    lines.append(line("N0 =", N0))
   237	    lines.append(line("N2 =", N2))
   238	    lines.append(line("N4 =", N4))
   239	    lines.append(
   240	        "We now perturb the primitive one-port data by small mouth-local projected-Maxwell slippages:\n"
   241	        "  Q -> Q + ell*q1,\n"
   242	        "  S2 -> S2 + ell*s1,\n"
   243	        "  H  -> H  + ell*h1,\n"
   244	        "  Delta -> Delta + ell*d1,\n"
   245	        "  P -> P + ell*p1,\n"
   246	        "  Gw -> Gw + ell*g1.\n"
   247	    )
   248	
   249	    lines.append(banner("2) Induced bundle corrections z0, z2, z4, n0, n2, n4"))
   250	    lines.append(line("z0 =", z0))
   251	    lines.append(line("z2 =", z2))
   252	    lines.append(line("z4 =", z4))
   253	    lines.append(line("n0 =", n0))
   254	    lines.append(line("n2 =", n2))
   255	    lines.append(line("n4 =", n4))
   256	
   257	    lines.append(banner("3) Reading the primitive slippages"))
   258	    lines.append(
   259	        "Three useful structural facts are immediate:\n"
   260	        "  1. z0 depends only on (q1, d1).\n"
   261	        "  2. z2 depends only on (q1, s1, h1, d1).\n"
   262	        "  3. n0 depends only on (p1, d1).\n"
   263	        "So the first isotropic normalization shift is controlled by the primitive transfer-leg slippage p1,\n"
   264	        "the primitive denominator slippage d1, and the primitive conservative source slippage q1.\n"
   265	    )
   266	
   267	    lines.append(banner("4) Direct primitive contribution to Xi1 = P1/P0"))
   268	    lines.append(line("Xi1^(primitive,static) =", Xi1_static))
   269	    lines.append(
   270	        "So at the one-port primitive level the weak-axisymmetric static prefactor slope receives contributions from:\n"
   271	        "  - transfer-leg slippage p1,\n"
   272	        "  - denominator slippage d1,\n"
   273	        "  - and direct conservative source slippage q1 through z0/D0.\n"
   274	    )
   275	
   276	    lines.append(banner("5) Direct primitive contribution to the isotropic compatibility shift"))
   277	    lines.append(line("delta Compat^(primitive) =", dCompat))
   278	    lines.append(
   279	        "Because delta Compat depends on z2, z4, and n0 only, the primitive contributors that matter here are\n"
   280	        "  q1, s1, h1, d1, p1,\n"
   281	        "while the Gw slope g1 first appears only through n2 and n4, i.e. in the constant-prefactor transport.\n"
   282	        "The script also checks that z0 cancels from both the fixed-target and transported-target compatibility shifts,\n"
   283	        "even though the normalization K surface still carries z0 before compatibility elimination.\n"
   284	    )
   285	
   286	    lines.append(banner("6) Practical reading"))
   287	    lines.append(
   288	        "At this one-port primitive level the projected-Maxwell near-throat correction has a clean hierarchy:\n"
   289	        "  - (q1, d1, p1) are the first static normalization movers,\n"
   290	        "  - (s1, h1) first matter through z2 and z4, hence through one-pole / compatibility transport,\n"
   291	        "  - g1 first matters for N2 and N4, hence for whether the branch stays on the constant-prefactor outgoing surface.\n"
   292	        "\n"
   293	        "This is a useful way to decide which concrete throat-local projected ansatz to try next.\n"
   294	    )
   295	
   296	    print("".join(lines))
   297	    print("STATUS: PASS")
   298

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba paper/stages/stage_022.tex | sed -n '1,260p'" in /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger

     1	\section[Stage 022]{Stage 022: Grouped Real \(P_2\) Normalization Bridge}
     2	\label{stage:022}
     3	
     4	\stagefield{Part / anchor}{Part I; primary anchors \resultanchor{MTDC-T3} and \resultanchor{MTDC-T4}.}
     5	\claimstatus{The bridge formulas are \StatusExactClosure{} inside the grouped response normalization convention.  The final equality to the universal normalization is a \StatusOpen{} branch-realization test.}
     6	
     7	\stagefield{Purpose}{Stage~022 translates the Stages~004--021 projection-first Maxwell packet, through its matched one-port outgoing normal form, into the grouped real \(P_2\) response language.  It converts conservative operator moments \(D_{An}\) into normalized response moments \(u_n^{(A)}\), tracks the outgoing-transfer prefactor \(P_{An}\), and isolates the exact invariant product that must match the universal quadrupole normalization.}
     8	
     9	\stagefield{Inputs}{The inputs are one conservative grouped-lane operator \(D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)\), one outgoing transfer factor \(N_A(\omega)\) exported by the matched Stage~021 one-port normal form, the compact outgoing \(l=2\) fingerprint, and the grouped trace/anomaly map.}
    10	
    11	\stagefield{Verification}{SymPy audit: \StageFile{scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_sympy_audit.py}; transcript: \StageFile{scripts/output/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_sympy_audit.txt}.  Mathematica audit: \StageFile{mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_normalization_bridge_mathematica_audit.wl}; transcript: \StageFile{mathematica/output/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_normalization_bridge_mathematica_audit.txt}.}
    12	
    13	\subsection*{Derivation}
    14	
    15	\paragraph{1. Conservative operator and normalized response.}
    16	For each grouped lane \(A\in\{20,21,22\}\), write
    17	\begin{equation}
    18	\label{eq:app-stage022-d-operator}
    19	D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)=D_{A0}+D_{A2}\omega^2+D_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    20	\end{equation}
    21	The normalized conservative response is
    22	\begin{equation}
    23	\label{eq:app-stage022-y-response}
    24	Y_A(\omega)=\frac{D_{A0}}{D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)}
    25	=1+u_2^{(A)}\omega^2+u_4^{(A)}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    26	\end{equation}
    27	Expanding the inverse gives
    28	\begin{equation}
    29	\label{eq:app-stage022-u2u4}
    30	\boxed{
    31	u_2^{(A)}=-\frac{D_{A2}}{D_{A0}},
    32	\qquad
    33	u_4^{(A)}=\frac{D_{A2}^2-D_{A0}D_{A4}}{D_{A0}^2}}.
    34	\end{equation}
    35	Here the symbol \(u_n^{(A)}\) is used in the main ledger; the boxed formula records the same coefficients.  The key point is that the normalized response moments are not independent of the operator moments.
    36	
    37	\paragraph{2. Grouped inverse map.}
    38	For any grouped triple \(x_A=(x_{20},x_{21},x_{22})\), define
    39	\begin{equation}
    40	\label{eq:app-stage022-grouped-defs}
    41	\bar x=\frac{x_{20}+2x_{21}+2x_{22}}{5},
    42	\qquad
    43	a_x=\frac{2x_{20}-x_{21}-x_{22}}{10},
    44	\qquad
    45	b_x=\frac{x_{21}-x_{22}}{2}.
    46	\end{equation}
    47	The inverse map is
    48	\begin{equation}
    49	\label{eq:app-stage022-grouped-inverse}
    50	\boxed{
    51	x_{20}=\bar x+4a_x,
    52	\qquad
    53	x_{21}=\bar x-a_x+b_x,
    54	\qquad
    55	x_{22}=\bar x-a_x-b_x}.
    56	\end{equation}
    57	The grouped isotropy gate is
    58	\begin{equation}
    59	\label{eq:app-stage022-isotropy-gate}
    60	a_2=b_2=a_4=b_4=0
    61	\end{equation}
    62	when applied to \(u_2^{(A)}\) and \(u_4^{(A)}\).
    63	
    64	\paragraph{3. Internal outgoing prefactor.}
    65	Let
    66	\begin{equation}
    67	\label{eq:app-stage022-n-transfer}
    68	N_A(\omega)=N_{A0}+N_{A2}\omega^2+N_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    69	\end{equation}
    70	To first order in the outgoing branch, the normalized response correction is controlled by
    71	\begin{equation}
    72	\label{eq:app-stage022-pref-def}
    73	{\rm Pref}_A(\omega)
    74	=\frac{D_{A0}N_A(\omega)}{\left(D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)\right)^2}
    75	=P_{A0}+P_{A2}\omega^2+P_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    76	\end{equation}
    77	Direct expansion gives
    78	\begin{align}
    79	\label{eq:app-stage022-pref-coeffs}
    80	\boxed{P_{A0}}&=\boxed{\frac{N_{A0}}{D_{A0}}},\\
    81	\boxed{P_{A2}}&=\boxed{\frac{D_{A0}N_{A2}-2D_{A2}N_{A0}}{D_{A0}^2}},\\
    82	\boxed{P_{A4}}&=\boxed{\frac{D_{A0}^2N_{A4}-2D_{A0}(D_{A2}N_{A2}+D_{A4}N_{A0})+3D_{A2}^2N_{A0}}{D_{A0}^3}}.
    83	\end{align}
    84	These are the algebraic bridge from conservative internal data to outgoing-branch prefactor data.
    85	
    86	\paragraph{4. Multiplication by the compact outgoing fingerprint.}
    87	Write the compact outgoing branch as
    88	\begin{equation}
    89	\label{eq:app-stage022-outgoing-compact}
    90	\widehat Y_2^{\rm out}(\omega)
    91	=1+A\omega^2+B\omega^4+iG_5\omega^5+O(\omega^6),
    92	\end{equation}
    93	with
    94	\begin{equation}
    95	\label{eq:app-stage022-abg}
    96	A=\frac{a^2}{9c_s^2},
    97	\qquad
    98	B=\frac{4a^4}{81c_s^4},
    99	\qquad
   100	G_5=\frac{a^5}{27c_s^5}.
   101	\end{equation}
   102	Then
   103	\begin{equation}
   104	\delta Y_A^{\rm out}(\omega)={\rm Pref}_A(\omega)\widehat Y_2^{\rm out}(\omega)
   105	=K_{A0}+K_{A2}\omega^2+K_{A4}\omega^4+i\Gamma_5^{(A)}\omega^5+\cdots,
   106	\end{equation}
   107	where
   108	\begin{align}
   109	\label{eq:app-stage022-k-gamma}
   110	K_{A0}&=P_{A0},\\
   111	K_{A2}&=P_{A2}+AP_{A0},\\
   112	K_{A4}&=P_{A4}+AP_{A2}+BP_{A0},\\
   113	\Gamma_5^{(A)}&=G_5P_{A0}.
   114	\end{align}
   115	Thus only the static prefactor \(P_{A0}\) enters the leading odd \(i\omega^5\) coefficient.
   116	
   117	\paragraph{5. Universal normalization product.}
   118	The GR quadrupole target is
   119	\begin{equation}
   120	\label{eq:app-stage022-gamma-gr}
   121	\gamma_{\rm GR}=\frac{2G}{5c^5}.
   122	\end{equation}
   123	Including the source-map factor \(\widehat m_0\), the invariant normalization condition is
   124	\begin{equation}
   125	\label{eq:app-stage022-invariant-normalization}
   126	\boxed{
   127	\widehat m_0^{\,2}P_0\frac{a^5}{27c_s^5}=\frac{2G}{5c^5}},
   128	\end{equation}
   129	or equivalently
   130	\begin{equation}
   131	\label{eq:app-stage022-p0-target}
   132	\boxed{
   133	\widehat m_0^{\,2}P_0=\frac{54Gc_s^5}{5a^5c^5}}.
   134	\end{equation}
   135	On the leading natural source-map branch \(\widehat m_0=1+O(a^2/r^2)\), this reduces to a direct target for \(P_0\).
   136	
   137	\stagefield{Output}{Stage~022 outputs the normalized response formulas \eqref{eq:app-stage022-u2u4}, the grouped inverse map \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-inverse}, the prefactor coefficients \eqref{eq:app-stage022-pref-coeffs}, the odd coefficient \eqref{eq:app-stage022-k-gamma}, and the invariant normalization test \eqref{eq:app-stage022-p0-target}.}
   138	
   139	\stagefield{Checks}{\begin{verificationchecklist}
   140	\item The inverse expansion of \(D_{A0}/D_A\) gives \(u_2,u_4\) with the signs shown in \eqref{eq:app-stage022-u2u4}.
   141	\item The grouped inverse map \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-inverse} reproduces \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-defs} exactly.
   142	\item The first odd coefficient depends only on \(P_{A0}\), because \({\rm Pref}_A\) is even through the retained orders and the outgoing odd term first appears at \(\omega^5\).
   143	\item The final equality \eqref{eq:app-stage022-p0-target} is a branch test, not a proof of branch realization.
   144	\end{verificationchecklist}}
   145	
   146	\stagefield{Downstream use}{Stage~023 uses the Stage~022 bridge to define \(P_0=N_0/D_0\) for the full grouped bundle.  The 2.5PN and 4PN bridge sections use \eqref{eq:app-stage022-p0-target} as the shared outgoing-normalization condition.  Later same-charge and weak-axisymmetric stages use the ratio \(P_1/P_0\) as the transported prefactor slope.}

     1	\section[Stage 023]{Stage 023: Full Grouped Bundle, Exact Projectors, and Isotropic Normalization Test}

     3

     5	\claimstatus{The grouped projectors, coefficient formulas, isotropic reduction, constant-prefactor conditions, and first-order transport laws are \StatusExactClosure{} inside the full reduced grouped-bundle model.  Actual values of the microscopic branch data remain \StatusOpen{}.}
     6	
     7	\stagefield{Purpose}{Stage~023 writes the entire grouped real \(20/21/22\) bundle in one place.  It combines the wall baseline, BdG support moments, projection-first Maxwell/matched mixed conservative moments, and outgoing-transfer moments; introduces exact weighted projectors for the grouped lanes; states the isotropic normalization test in full-bundle variables; and derives the first-order anisotropy transport laws.}
     8	
     9	\stagefield{Inputs}{The inputs are the Stage~003 BdG moments, the Stages~004--021 projected Maxwell-to-\(Z_n,N_n\) packet and matched one-port conservative/transfer moments, the Stage~022 response/prefactor conversion, and the grouped metric \(G_{\rm grp}=\operatorname{diag}(1,2,2)\).}

Mathematica audit: \StageFile{mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage023_full_grouped_bundle_mathematica_audit.wl}; transcript: \StageFile{mathematica/output/moving_throat_pde_stage023_full_grouped_bundle_mathematica_audit.txt}.}
    12	
    13	\subsection*{Derivation}
    14	
    15	\paragraph{1. Weighted grouped projector calculus.}
    16	The grouped triple \((20,21,22)\) carries metric
    17	\begin{equation}
    18	\label{eq:app-stage023-grouped-metric}
    19	G_{\rm grp}=\operatorname{diag}(1,2,2).
    20	\end{equation}
    21	Use the \(G_{\rm grp}\)-orthogonal basis
    22	\begin{equation}
    23	\label{eq:app-stage023-basis}
    24	e_{\bar x}=(1,1,1),
    25	\qquad
    26	e_a=(4,-1,-1),
    27	\qquad
    28	e_b=(0,1,-1).
    29	\end{equation}
    30	The norms are
    31	\begin{equation}
    32	\label{eq:app-stage023-norms}
    33	e_{\bar x}^TG_{\rm grp}e_{\bar x}=5,
    34	\qquad
    35	e_a^TG_{\rm grp}e_a=20,
    36	\qquad
    37	e_b^TG_{\rm grp}e_b=4.
    38	\end{equation}
    39	Thus any grouped vector decomposes as
    40	\begin{equation}
    41	\label{eq:app-stage023-decomp}
    42	x=\bar x\,e_{\bar x}+a_xe_a+b_xe_b,
    43	\end{equation}
    44	with \(\bar x,a_x,b_x\) as in \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-defs}.  The exact projectors are
    45	\begin{equation}
    46	\label{eq:app-stage023-projectors}
    47	\boxed{
    48	P_{\bar x}=\frac15
    49	\begin{pmatrix}
    50	1&2&2\\1&2&2\\1&2&2
    51	\end{pmatrix},
    52	\quad
    53	P_a=\frac1{20}
    54	\begin{pmatrix}
    55	16&-8&-8\\-4&2&2\\-4&2&2
    56	\end{pmatrix},
    57	\quad
    58	P_b=\frac14
    59	\begin{pmatrix}
    60	0&0&0\\0&2&-2\\0&-2&2
    61	\end{pmatrix}}.
    62	\end{equation}
    63	They obey
    64	\begin{equation}
    65	\label{eq:app-stage023-projector-identities}
    66	P_{\bar x}+P_a+P_b=I_3,
    67	\qquad
    68	P_iP_j=\delta_{ij}P_i.
    69	\end{equation}
    70	Grouped isotropy is the condition
    71	\begin{equation}
    72	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropy-projector}
    73	P_ax=P_bx=0.
    74	\end{equation}
    75	
    76	\paragraph{2. Full coupled lane Lagrangian.}
    77	For each lane \(A\in\{20,21,22\}\), retain a wall amplitude \(q_A\), stable BdG support modes \(X_{A\alpha}\), brane-like gauge coordinates \(U_{A r}\), mixed coordinates \(W_{A r}\), and internal gauge mixing \(R_{A r}\).  The reduced Lagrangian is
    78	\begin{align}
    79	\label{eq:app-stage023-full-lagrangian}
    80	L_A={}&\frac12M_A\dot q_A^2-\frac12K_Aq_A^2
    81	+\sum_\alpha\left[\frac12\dot X_{A\alpha}^2-\frac12\varpi_{A\alpha}^2X_{A\alpha}^2+c_{A\alpha}q_AX_{A\alpha}\right]\\
    82	&+\sum_r\bigl[\frac12\dot U_{A r}^2-\frac12\Omega_{U,A r}^2U_{A r}^2
    83	+\frac12\dot W_{A r}^2-\frac12\Omega_{W,A r}^2W_{A r}^2
    84	+R_{A r}U_{A r}W_{A r}\\
    85	&\qquad\quad
    86	+g_{U,A r}q_AU_{A r}+g_{W,A r}q_AW_{A r}\bigr].
    87	\end{align}
    88	
    89	\paragraph{3. BdG support moments.}
    90	Define
    91	\begin{equation}
    92	\label{eq:app-stage023-b-moments}
    93	\boxed{
    94	B_{A0}=\sum_\alpha\frac{c_{A\alpha}^2}{\varpi_{A\alpha}^2},
    95	\qquad
    96	B_{A2}=\sum_\alpha\frac{c_{A\alpha}^2}{\varpi_{A\alpha}^4},
    97	\qquad
    98	B_{A4}=\sum_\alpha\frac{c_{A\alpha}^2}{\varpi_{A\alpha}^6}}.
    99	\end{equation}
   100	These are the Stage~003 Schur-complement moments collected lane by lane.
   101	
   102	\paragraph{4. Conservative Maxwell/mixed moments.}
   103	For each port \(r\), set
   104	\begin{equation}
   105	\label{eq:app-stage023-delta-s-q-g}
   106	\Delta_{A r}=\Omega_{U,A r}^2\Omega_{W,A r}^2-R_{A r}^2,
   107	\qquad
   108	S_{A r}=\Omega_{U,A r}^2+\Omega_{W,A r}^2,
   109	\end{equation}
   110	\begin{equation}
   111	Q_{A r}=g_{U,A r}^2\Omega_{W,A r}^2
   112	+2g_{U,A r}g_{W,A r}R_{A r}
   113	+g_{W,A r}^2\Omega_{U,A r}^2,
   114	\qquad
   115	G_{A r}=g_{U,A r}^2+g_{W,A r}^2.
   116	\end{equation}
   117	The conservative Maxwell/mixed moments are
   118	\begin{align}
   119	\label{eq:app-stage023-z-moments-port}
   120	Z_{A0}^{(r)}&=\frac{Q_{A r}}{\Delta_{A r}},\\
   121	Z_{A2}^{(r)}&=\frac{Q_{A r}S_{A r}-G_{A r}\Delta_{A r}}{\Delta_{A r}^2},\\
   122	Z_{A4}^{(r)}&=\frac{Q_{A r}(S_{A r}^2-\Delta_{A r})-S_{A r}G_{A r}\Delta_{A r}}{\Delta_{A r}^3},
   123	\end{align}
   124	and
   125	\begin{equation}
   126	\label{eq:app-stage023-z-sum}
   127	Z_{An}=\sum_rZ_{An}^{(r)},
   128	\qquad
   129	n\in\{0,2,4\}.
   130	\end{equation}
   131	
   132	\paragraph{5. Outgoing-transfer moments.}
   133	Define
   134	\begin{equation}
   135	\label{eq:app-stage023-p-ar}
   136	P_{A r}=\Omega_{U,A r}^2g_{W,A r}+R_{A r}g_{U,A r}.
   137	\end{equation}
   138	The outgoing-transfer moments per port are
   139	\begin{align}
   140	\label{eq:app-stage023-n-moments-port}
   141	N_{A0}^{(r)}&=\frac{P_{A r}^2}{\Delta_{A r}^2},\\
   142	N_{A2}^{(r)}&=\frac{2P_{A r}\bigl(P_{A r}S_{A r}-\Delta_{A r}g_{W,A r}\bigr)}{\Delta_{A r}^3},\\
   143	N_{A4}^{(r)}&=\frac{\Delta_{A r}^2g_{W,A r}^2-2\Delta_{A r}P_{A r}^2-4\Delta_{A r}P_{A r}S_{A r}g_{W,A r}+3P_{A r}^2S_{A r}^2}{\Delta_{A r}^4}.
   144	\end{align}
   145	Summing over outgoing ports gives
   146	\begin{equation}
   147	\label{eq:app-stage023-n-sum}
   148	N_{An}=\sum_rN_{An}^{(r)},
   149	\qquad
   150	n\in\{0,2,4\}.
   151	\end{equation}
   152	
   153	\paragraph{6. Total conservative grouped operator.}
   154	The full conservative grouped-lane operator is
   155	\begin{equation}
   156	\label{eq:app-stage023-d-total}
   157	D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)=D_{A0}+D_{A2}\omega^2+D_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6),
   158	\end{equation}
   159	with
   160	\begin{equation}
   161	\label{eq:app-stage023-d-coeffs}
   162	\boxed{
   163	D_{A0}=K_A-B_{A0}-Z_{A0},
   164	\qquad
   165	D_{A2}=-\bigl(M_A+B_{A2}+Z_{A2}\bigr),
   166	\qquad
   167	D_{A4}=-\bigl(B_{A4}+Z_{A4}\bigr)}.
   168	\end{equation}
   169	This is the full Part-I coefficient ledger.
   170	
   171	\paragraph{7. Microscopic anisotropy ledger.}
   172	Apply the grouped decomposition \eqref{eq:app-stage023-decomp} to every microscopic family \(K_A,M_A,B_{An},Z_{An},N_{An}\).  The static conservative defects obey
   173	\begin{equation}
   174	\label{eq:app-stage023-d0-defects}
   175	\bar D_0=\bar K-\bar B_0-\bar Z_0,
   176	\qquad
   177	a_{D,0}=a_K-a_{B,0}-a_{Z,0},
   178	\qquad
   179	b_{D,0}=b_K-b_{B,0}-b_{Z,0}.
   180	\end{equation}
   181	Similarly,
   182	\begin{equation}
   183	\label{eq:app-stage023-d2-defects}
   184	\bar D_2=-(\bar M+\bar B_2+\bar Z_2),
   185	\qquad
   186	a_{D,2}=-(a_M+a_{B,2}+a_{Z,2}),
   187	\qquad
   188	b_{D,2}=-(b_M+b_{B,2}+b_{Z,2}),
   189	\end{equation}
   190	and
   191	\begin{equation}
   192	\label{eq:app-stage023-d4-defects}
   193	\bar D_4=-(\bar B_4+\bar Z_4),
   194	\qquad
   195	a_{D,4}=-(a_{B,4}+a_{Z,4}),
   196	\qquad
   197	b_{D,4}=-(b_{B,4}+b_{Z,4}).
   198	\end{equation}
   199	Thus grouped anisotropy can enter only through the wall baseline, the BdG support bundle, or the Maxwell/mixed conservative and outgoing-transfer bundles.
   200	
   201	\paragraph{8. Isotropic branch and normalization ratio.}
   202	On the isotropic branch,
   203	\begin{equation}
   204	D_{20,n}=D_{21,n}=D_{22,n}=D_n,
   205	\qquad
   206	N_{20,n}=N_{21,n}=N_{22,n}=N_n.
   207	\end{equation}
   208	Stage~022 then gives
   209	\begin{equation}
   210	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-response}
   211	u_2=-\frac{D_2}{D_0},
   212	\qquad
   213	u_4=\frac{D_2^2-D_0D_4}{D_0^2},
   214	\end{equation}
   215	and
   216	\begin{equation}
   217	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-pref}
   218	P_0=\frac{N_0}{D_0},
   219	\qquad
   220	P_2=\frac{D_0N_2-2D_2N_0}{D_0^2},
   221	\end{equation}
   222	\begin{equation}
   223	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-p4}
   224	P_4=\frac{D_0^2N_4-2D_0(D_2N_2+D_4N_0)+3D_2^2N_0}{D_0^3}.
   225	\end{equation}
   226	The isotropic normalization test is
   227	\begin{equation}
   228	\label{eq:app-stage023-normalization-test}
   229	\boxed{
   230	\widehat m_0^{\,2}\frac{N_0}{K-B_0-Z_0}
   231	=\frac{54Gc_s^5}{5a^5c^5}}.
   232	\end{equation}
   233	The denominator is the static full-bundle stiffness
   234	\begin{equation}
   235	D_0=K-B_0-Z_0.
   236	\end{equation}
   237	
   238	\paragraph{9. Constant-prefactor branch.}
   239	The constant-prefactor branch through the retained orders is
   240	\begin{equation}
   241	P_2=0,
   242	\qquad
   243	P_4=0.
   244	\end{equation}
   245	Using \eqref{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-pref}--\eqref{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-p4}, this is equivalent to
   246	\begin{equation}
   247	\label{eq:app-stage023-constant-prefactor-conditions}
   248	\boxed{
   249	N_2=\frac{2D_2N_0}{D_0},
   250	\qquad
   251	N_4=\frac{2D_0(D_2N_2+D_4N_0)-3D_2^2N_0}{D_0^2}}.
   252	\end{equation}
   253	So constant outgoing prefactor does not require \(N_2=N_4=0\); it requires precise correlation between transfer and conservative moments.
   254	
   255	\paragraph{10. First-order anisotropy transport.}
   256	Let
   257	\begin{equation}
   258	D_{An}=D_n+\delta D_{An},
   259	\qquad
   260	N_{An}=N_n+\delta N_{An},
   261	\end{equation}
   262	near the isotropic branch.  From \(u_2^{(A)}=-D_{A2}/D_{A0}\),
   263	\begin{equation}
   264	\label{eq:app-stage023-du2}
   265	\delta u_2^{(A)}=-\frac{\delta D_{A2}+u_2\delta D_{A0}}{D_0}.
   266	\end{equation}
   267	Therefore
   268	\begin{equation}
   269	\label{eq:app-stage023-u-anisotropy}
   270	\boxed{
   271	a_{u,2}=-\frac{a_{D,2}+u_2a_{D,0}}{D_0},
   272	\qquad
   273	b_{u,2}=-\frac{b_{D,2}+u_2b_{D,0}}{D_0}}.
   274	\end{equation}
   275	Similarly, from \(P_0^{(A)}=N_{A0}/D_{A0}\),
   276	\begin{equation}
   277	\label{eq:app-stage023-dp0}
   278	\delta P_0^{(A)}=\frac{\delta N_{A0}-P_0\delta D_{A0}}{D_0},
   279	\end{equation}
   280	so

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""
     3	moving_throat_pde_stage6_full_grouped_bundle_sympy_audit.py
     4	
     5	SymPy audit for Stage 6 of the moving-throat PDE program.
     6	
     7	Scope
     8	-----
     9	This script verifies the first exact full-bundle bookkeeping layer for the grouped
    10	real P2 system:
    11	
    12	  • the weighted grouped metric and exact projectors,
    13	  • the exact decomposition x = xbar ebar + a ea + b eb,
    14	  • the full coupled bundle assembly of D_A0, D_A2, D_A4 from wall + BdG +
    15	    conservative Maxwell/mixed data,
    16	  • the exact grouped trace/anomaly decomposition of those microscopic coefficients,
    17	  • the isotropic-branch formulas for u2, u4, P0, P2, P4,
    18	  • the exact normalization ratio mhat^2 N0/(K-B0-Z0),
    19	  • the constant-prefactor branch conditions,
    20	  • first-order anisotropy transport laws for u2 and P0,
    21	  • and simple monotonicity derivatives of the isotropic normalization ratio.
    22	
    23	This is still a reduced-sector audit. It does not solve the full moving-throat PDE,
    24	but it does verify the exact algebra needed for the next theorem gate.
    25	"""
    26	
    27	from __future__ import annotations
    28	
    29	import sympy as sp
    30	
    31	I = sp.I
    32	
    33	
    34	def banner(title: str) -> None:
    35	    line = "=" * 88
    36	    print("\n" + line)
    37	    print(title)
    38	    print(line)
    39	
    40	
    41	def subbanner(title: str) -> None:
    42	    line = "-" * 88
    43	    print("\n" + line)
    44	    print(title)
    45	    print(line)
    46	
    47	
    48	def expect_zero(name: str, expr: sp.Expr | sp.Matrix) -> None:
    49	    if isinstance(expr, sp.MatrixBase):
    50	        simplified = expr.applyfunc(lambda z: sp.simplify(sp.expand(z)))
    51	        print(f"{name} =")
    52	        sp.pprint(simplified)
    53	        if any(entry != 0 for entry in simplified):
    54	            raise AssertionError(f"{name} is not zero")
    55	    else:
    56	        simplified = sp.simplify(sp.expand(expr))
    57	        print(f"{name} = {simplified}")
    58	        if simplified != 0:
    59	            raise AssertionError(f"{name} is not zero")
    60	
    61	
    62	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63	# I. Weighted grouped-metric projector calculus
    64	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65	
    66	def grouped_projector_calculus() -> None:
    67	    banner("SECTION I — WEIGHTED GROUPED-METRIC PROJECTOR CALCULUS")
    68	
    69	    Ggrp = sp.diag(1, 2, 2)
    70	    ebar = sp.Matrix([1, 1, 1])
    71	    ea = sp.Matrix([4, -1, -1])
    72	    eb = sp.Matrix([0, 1, -1])
    73	
    74	    subbanner("I.1 — Ggrp-orthogonality and norms")
    75	    expect_zero("ebar^T G ea", (ebar.T * Ggrp * ea)[0])
    76	    expect_zero("ebar^T G eb", (ebar.T * Ggrp * eb)[0])
    77	    expect_zero("ea^T G eb", (ea.T * Ggrp * eb)[0])
    78	    expect_zero("||ebar||_G^2 - 5", (ebar.T * Ggrp * ebar)[0] - 5)
    79	    expect_zero("||ea||_G^2 - 20", (ea.T * Ggrp * ea)[0] - 20)
    80	    expect_zero("||eb||_G^2 - 4", (eb.T * Ggrp * eb)[0] - 4)
    81	
    82	    Pbar = sp.simplify(ebar * (ebar.T * Ggrp) / 5)
    83	    Pa = sp.simplify(ea * (ea.T * Ggrp) / 20)
    84	    Pb = sp.simplify(eb * (eb.T * Ggrp) / 4)
    85	
    86	    subbanner("I.2 — Exact projectors")
    87	    print("Pbar =")
    88	    sp.pprint(Pbar)
    89	    print("Pa =")
    90	    sp.pprint(Pa)
    91	    print("Pb =")
    92	    sp.pprint(Pb)
    93	
    94	    expect_zero("Pbar^2 - Pbar", Pbar * Pbar - Pbar)
    95	    expect_zero("Pa^2 - Pa", Pa * Pa - Pa)
    96	    expect_zero("Pb^2 - Pb", Pb * Pb - Pb)
    97	    expect_zero("Pbar Pa", Pbar * Pa)
    98	    expect_zero("Pbar Pb", Pbar * Pb)
    99	    expect_zero("Pa Pb", Pa * Pb)
   100	    expect_zero("Pbar + Pa + Pb - I", Pbar + Pa + Pb - sp.eye(3))
   101	
   102	    x20, x21, x22 = sp.symbols("x20 x21 x22", real=True)
   103	    x = sp.Matrix([x20, x21, x22])
   104	    xbar = sp.simplify((x20 + 2 * x21 + 2 * x22) / 5)
   105	    ax = sp.simplify((2 * x20 - x21 - x22) / 10)
   106	    bx = sp.simplify((x21 - x22) / 2)
   107	
   108	    subbanner("I.3 — Exact decomposition of an arbitrary grouped vector")
   109	    expect_zero("Pbar*x - xbar*ebar", Pbar * x - xbar * ebar)
   110	    expect_zero("Pa*x - ax*ea", Pa * x - ax * ea)
   111	    expect_zero("Pb*x - bx*eb", Pb * x - bx * eb)
   112	    expect_zero("x - (xbar ebar + ax ea + bx eb)", x - (xbar * ebar + ax * ea + bx * eb))
   113	
   114	
   115	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116	# II. Full coupled bundle coefficient assembly
   117	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118	
   119	def bundle_coefficient_assembly() -> dict[str, sp.Expr]:
   120	    banner("SECTION II — FULL COUPLED BUNDLE COEFFICIENT ASSEMBLY")
   121	
   122	    subbanner("II.0 — Representative one-port derivation of Z_n and N_n")
   123	    omega = sp.symbols("omega", real=True)
   124	    OmegaU, OmegaW, Rmix, gU, gW = sp.symbols("Omega_U Omega_W R_mix g_U g_W", real=True)
   125	
   126	    Delta_expr = sp.simplify(OmegaU**2 * OmegaW**2 - Rmix**2)
   127	    S_expr = sp.simplify(OmegaU**2 + OmegaW**2)
   128	    Q_expr = sp.simplify(gU**2 * OmegaW**2 + 2 * gU * gW * Rmix + gW**2 * OmegaU**2)
   129	    H_expr = sp.simplify(gU**2 + gW**2)
   130	    P_expr = sp.simplify(OmegaU**2 * gW + Rmix * gU)
   131	
   132	    Z_one_port = sp.expand(
   133	        sp.series(
   134	            (Q_expr - H_expr * omega**2) / (Delta_expr - S_expr * omega**2 + omega**4),
   135	            omega,
   136	            0,
   137	            6,
   138	        ).removeO()
   139	    )
   140	    expect_zero("Z0 one-port formula", Z_one_port.coeff(omega, 0) - Q_expr / Delta_expr)
   141	    expect_zero(
   142	        "Z2 one-port formula",
   143	        Z_one_port.coeff(omega, 2) - (Q_expr * S_expr - H_expr * Delta_expr) / Delta_expr**2,
   144	    )
   145	    expect_zero(
   146	        "Z4 one-port formula",
   147	        Z_one_port.coeff(omega, 4)
   148	        - (Q_expr * (S_expr**2 - Delta_expr) - S_expr * H_expr * Delta_expr) / Delta_expr**3,
   149	    )
   150	
   151	    N_one_port = sp.expand(
   152	        sp.series(
   153	            (P_expr - gW * omega**2) ** 2 / (Delta_expr - S_expr * omega**2 + omega**4) ** 2,
   154	            omega,
   155	            0,
   156	            6,
   157	        ).removeO()
   158	    )
   159	    expect_zero("N0 one-port formula", N_one_port.coeff(omega, 0) - P_expr**2 / Delta_expr**2)
   160	    expect_zero(
   161	        "N2 one-port formula",
   162	        N_one_port.coeff(omega, 2)
   163	        - 2 * P_expr * (P_expr * S_expr - Delta_expr * gW) / Delta_expr**3,
   164	    )
   165	    expect_zero(
   166	        "N4 one-port formula",
   167	        N_one_port.coeff(omega, 4)
   168	        - (
   169	            Delta_expr**2 * gW**2
   170	            - 2 * Delta_expr * P_expr**2
   171	            - 4 * Delta_expr * P_expr * S_expr * gW
   172	            + 3 * P_expr**2 * S_expr**2
   173	        )
   174	        / Delta_expr**4,
   175	    )
   176	
   177	    # Lane symbols for the three grouped channels.
   178	    K20, K21, K22 = sp.symbols("K20 K21 K22", real=True)
   179	    M20, M21, M22 = sp.symbols("M20 M21 M22", real=True)
   180	
   181	    # Stage-003 carry-forward: B_{A0}, B_{A2}, B_{A4} are the stable-BdG Schur
   182	    # sums sum_alpha g_{A alpha}^2 / varpi_{A alpha}^{2,4,6} in grouped notation.
   183	    B020, B021, B022 = sp.symbols("B0_20 B0_21 B0_22", real=True)
   184	    B220, B221, B222 = sp.symbols("B2_20 B2_21 B2_22", real=True)
   185	    B420, B421, B422 = sp.symbols("B4_20 B4_21 B4_22", real=True)
   186	
   187	    Z020, Z021, Z022 = sp.symbols("Z0_20 Z0_21 Z0_22", real=True)
   188	    Z220, Z221, Z222 = sp.symbols("Z2_20 Z2_21 Z2_22", real=True)
   189	    Z420, Z421, Z422 = sp.symbols("Z4_20 Z4_21 Z4_22", real=True)
   190	
   191	    N020, N021, N022 = sp.symbols("N0_20 N0_21 N0_22", real=True)
   192	    N220, N221, N222 = sp.symbols("N2_20 N2_21 N2_22", real=True)
   193	    N420, N421, N422 = sp.symbols("N4_20 N4_21 N4_22", real=True)
   194	
   195	    D020 = sp.simplify(K20 - B020 - Z020)
   196	    D021 = sp.simplify(K21 - B021 - Z021)
   197	    D022 = sp.simplify(K22 - B022 - Z022)
   198	
   199	    D220 = sp.simplify(-(M20 + B220 + Z220))
   200	    D221 = sp.simplify(-(M21 + B221 + Z221))
   201	    D222 = sp.simplify(-(M22 + B222 + Z222))
   202	
   203	    D420 = sp.simplify(-(B420 + Z420))
   204	    D421 = sp.simplify(-(B421 + Z421))
   205	    D422 = sp.simplify(-(B422 + Z422))
   206	
   207	    def grouped_parts(x20: sp.Expr, x21: sp.Expr, x22: sp.Expr) -> tuple[sp.Expr, sp.Expr, sp.Expr]:
   208	        xbar = sp.simplify((x20 + 2 * x21 + 2 * x22) / 5)
   209	        ax = sp.simplify((2 * x20 - x21 - x22) / 10)
   210	        bx = sp.simplify((x21 - x22) / 2)
   211	        return xbar, ax, bx
   212	
   213	    Kbar, aK, bK = grouped_parts(K20, K21, K22)
   214	    Mbar, aM, bM = grouped_parts(M20, M21, M22)
   215	
   216	    Bbar0, aB0, bB0 = grouped_parts(B020, B021, B022)
   217	    Bbar2, aB2, bB2 = grouped_parts(B220, B221, B222)
   218	    Bbar4, aB4, bB4 = grouped_parts(B420, B421, B422)
   219	
   220	    Zbar0, aZ0, bZ0 = grouped_parts(Z020, Z021, Z022)
   221	    Zbar2, aZ2, bZ2 = grouped_parts(Z220, Z221, Z222)
   222	    Zbar4, aZ4, bZ4 = grouped_parts(Z420, Z421, Z422)
   223	
   224	    Nbar0, aN0, bN0 = grouped_parts(N020, N021, N022)
   225	    Nbar2, aN2, bN2 = grouped_parts(N220, N221, N222)
   226	    Nbar4, aN4, bN4 = grouped_parts(N420, N421, N422)
   227	
   228	    Dbar0, aD0, bD0 = grouped_parts(D020, D021, D022)
   229	    Dbar2, aD2, bD2 = grouped_parts(D220, D221, D222)
   230	    Dbar4, aD4, bD4 = grouped_parts(D420, D421, D422)
   231	
   232	    subbanner("II.1 — Exact grouped decomposition of the coupled conservative coefficients")
   233	    expect_zero("Dbar0 - (Kbar - Bbar0 - Zbar0)", Dbar0 - (Kbar - Bbar0 - Zbar0))
   234	    expect_zero("aD0 - (aK - aB0 - aZ0)", aD0 - (aK - aB0 - aZ0))
   235	    expect_zero("bD0 - (bK - bB0 - bZ0)", bD0 - (bK - bB0 - bZ0))
   236	
   237	    expect_zero("Dbar2 + (Mbar + Bbar2 + Zbar2)", Dbar2 + (Mbar + Bbar2 + Zbar2))
   238	    expect_zero("aD2 + (aM + aB2 + aZ2)", aD2 + (aM + aB2 + aZ2))
   239	    expect_zero("bD2 + (bM + bB2 + bZ2)", bD2 + (bM + bB2 + bZ2))
   240	
   241	    expect_zero("Dbar4 + (Bbar4 + Zbar4)", Dbar4 + (Bbar4 + Zbar4))
   242	    expect_zero("aD4 + (aB4 + aZ4)", aD4 + (aB4 + aZ4))
   243	    expect_zero("bD4 + (bB4 + bZ4)", bD4 + (bB4 + bZ4))
   244	
   245	    subbanner("II.2 — The same grouped decomposition applies directly to the outgoing transfer bundle")
   246	    # Nothing to prove beyond linearity, but verify a representative identity.
   247	    # Non-tautological linearity test: grouped_parts(N0 + N2 lane sum)
   248	    # equals the sum of the individual grouped_parts outputs.
   249	    Nbar02, aN02, bN02 = grouped_parts(N020 + N220, N021 + N221, N022 + N222)
   250	    expect_zero("Nbar0 + Nbar2 additivity", Nbar02 - (Nbar0 + Nbar2))
   251	    expect_zero("aN0 + aN2 additivity", aN02 - (aN0 + aN2))
   252	    expect_zero("bN0 + bN2 additivity", bN02 - (bN0 + bN2))
   253	
   254	    return {
   255	        "Dbar0": Dbar0,
   256	        "Dbar2": Dbar2,
   257	        "Dbar4": Dbar4,
   258	        "Nbar0": Nbar0,
   259	        "Nbar2": Nbar2,
   260	        "Nbar4": Nbar4,
   261	    }
   262	
   263	
   264	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   265	# III. Isotropic branch, prefactors, and normalization target
   266	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   267	
   268	def isotropic_branch_and_target() -> dict[str, sp.Expr]:
   269	    banner("SECTION III — ISOTROPIC BRANCH, PREFACTORS, AND NORMALIZATION TARGET")
   270	
   271	    omega = sp.symbols("omega", real=True)
   272	    D0, D2, D4 = sp.symbols("D0 D2 D4", nonzero=True, real=True)
   273	    N0, N2, N4 = sp.symbols("N0 N2 N4", real=True)
   274	    G, c, c_s, a = sp.symbols("G c c_s a", positive=True, real=True)
   275	    mhat = sp.symbols("mhat", positive=True, real=True)
   276	
   277	    Dcons = D0 + D2 * omega**2 + D4 * omega**4
   278	    Yresp = sp.expand(sp.series(D0 / Dcons, omega, 0, 6).removeO())
   279	    u2 = sp.simplify(Yresp.coeff(omega, 2))
   280	    u4 = sp.simplify(Yresp.coeff(omega, 4))
   281	
   282	    Pref = sp.expand(sp.series(D0 * (N0 + N2 * omega**2 + N4 * omega**4) / Dcons**2, omega, 0, 6).removeO())
   283	    P0 = sp.simplify(Pref.coeff(omega, 0))
   284	    P2 = sp.simplify(Pref.coeff(omega, 2))
   285	    P4 = sp.simplify(Pref.coeff(omega, 4))
   286	
   287	    subbanner("III.1 — Exact isotropic formulas")
   288	    expect_zero("u2 + D2/D0", u2 + D2 / D0)
   289	    expect_zero("u4 - (D2^2 - D0 D4)/D0^2", u4 - (D2**2 - D0 * D4) / D0**2)
   290	    expect_zero("P0 - N0/D0", P0 - N0 / D0)
   291	    expect_zero("P2 - (D0 N2 - 2 D2 N0)/D0^2", P2 - (D0 * N2 - 2 * D2 * N0) / D0**2)
   292	    expect_zero(
   293	        "P4 - exact formula",
   294	        P4 - (D0**2 * N4 - 2 * D0 * (D2 * N2 + D4 * N0) + 3 * D2**2 * N0) / D0**3,
   295	    )
   296	
   297	    subbanner("III.2 — Constant-prefactor branch conditions")
   298	    N2_target = sp.simplify(sp.solve(sp.Eq(P2, 0), N2)[0])
   299	    N4_target = sp.simplify(sp.solve(sp.Eq(P4.subs(N2, N2_target), 0), N4)[0])
   300	    print("If P2 = 0, then N2 =")
   301	    sp.pprint(N2_target)
   302	    print("If P2 = P4 = 0, then N4 =")
   303	    sp.pprint(N4_target)
   304	
   305	    # Independent closed-form derivation of N2_target and N4_target.
   306	    # If the solver disagrees with these closed forms, the substitution check
   307	    # would still pass tautologically; comparing to the closed form catches it.
   308	    N2_target_closed = 2 * D2 * N0 / D0
   309	    N4_target_closed = sp.simplify(N0 * (2 * D0 * D4 + D2**2) / D0**2)
   310	    expect_zero("N2_target closed form", sp.simplify(N2_target - N2_target_closed))
   311	    expect_zero("N4_target closed form", sp.simplify(N4_target - N4_target_closed))
   312	
   313	    subbanner("III.3 — Universal normalization product")
   314	    N0_target = sp.simplify(sp.solve(sp.Eq(mhat**2 * (N0/D0), 54 * G * c_s**5 / (5 * a**5 * c**5)), N0)[0])
   315	    # Substantive check: N0_target, when substituted into mhat^2 * P0, must
   316	    # reproduce the universal normalization 54 G c_s^5 / (5 a^5 c^5).
   317	    expect_zero(
   318	        "N0_target reproduces universal normalization",
   319	        (mhat**2 * (N0_target/D0)) - 54 * G * c_s**5 / (5 * a**5 * c**5),
   320	    )
   321	    # Carry the outgoing odd coefficient through the same exact Stage-4/5 DtN
   322	    # route used in Stage 022 instead of retyping a^5/(27 c_s^5).
   323	    z = sp.symbols("z", positive=True, real=True)
   324	    j2 = (sp.Rational(3, 1) / z**3 - sp.Rational(1, 1) / z) * sp.sin(z) - 3 * sp.cos(z) / z**2
   325	    y2 = -((sp.Rational(3, 1) / z**3 - sp.Rational(1, 1) / z) * sp.cos(z) + 3 * sp.sin(z) / z**2)
   326	    h2 = sp.simplify(j2 + I * y2)
   327	    Lambda2 = sp.simplify(omega * sp.diff(h2, z) / h2).subs(z, omega * a / c_s)
   328	    Lambda2_series = sp.series(Lambda2, omega, 0, 7).removeO()
   329	    Y2 = sp.simplify(sp.series(1 / Lambda2_series, omega, 0, 6).removeO())
   330	    Y2_static = sp.simplify(Y2.subs(omega, 0))
   331	    Y2_hat = sp.expand(sp.simplify(Y2 / Y2_static))
   332	    Gamma5_port = sp.simplify(Y2_hat.coeff(omega, 5) / I)
   333	    expect_zero("Stage-5 Gamma5_port anchor", Gamma5_port - a**5 / (27 * c_s**5))
   334	    gamma_GR = sp.simplify(2 * G / (5 * c**5))
   335	    ratio_target = sp.simplify(sp.solve(sp.Eq(mhat**2 * P0 * Gamma5_port, gamma_GR), P0)[0])
   336	    print("Gamma5_port =")
   337	    sp.pprint(Gamma5_port)
   338	    print("Required mhat^2 * P0 =")
   339	    sp.pprint(ratio_target)
   340	    expect_zero(

   238	\paragraph{9. Constant-prefactor branch.}
   239	The constant-prefactor branch through the retained orders is
   240	\begin{equation}
   241	P_2=0,
   242	\qquad
   243	P_4=0.
   244	\end{equation}
   245	Using \eqref{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-pref}--\eqref{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-p4}, this is equivalent to
   246	\begin{equation}
   247	\label{eq:app-stage023-constant-prefactor-conditions}
   248	\boxed{
   249	N_2=\frac{2D_2N_0}{D_0},
   250	\qquad
   251	N_4=\frac{2D_0(D_2N_2+D_4N_0)-3D_2^2N_0}{D_0^2}}.
   252	\end{equation}
   253	So constant outgoing prefactor does not require \(N_2=N_4=0\); it requires precise correlation between transfer and conservative moments.
   254	
   255	\paragraph{10. First-order anisotropy transport.}
   256	Let
   257	\begin{equation}
   258	D_{An}=D_n+\delta D_{An},
   259	\qquad
   260	N_{An}=N_n+\delta N_{An},
   261	\end{equation}
   262	near the isotropic branch.  From \(u_2^{(A)}=-D_{A2}/D_{A0}\),
   263	\begin{equation}
   264	\label{eq:app-stage023-du2}
   265	\delta u_2^{(A)}=-\frac{\delta D_{A2}+u_2\delta D_{A0}}{D_0}.
   266	\end{equation}
   267	Therefore
   268	\begin{equation}
   269	\label{eq:app-stage023-u-anisotropy}
   270	\boxed{
   271	a_{u,2}=-\frac{a_{D,2}+u_2a_{D,0}}{D_0},
   272	\qquad
   273	b_{u,2}=-\frac{b_{D,2}+u_2b_{D,0}}{D_0}}.
   274	\end{equation}
   275	Similarly, from \(P_0^{(A)}=N_{A0}/D_{A0}\),
   276	\begin{equation}
   277	\label{eq:app-stage023-dp0}
   278	\delta P_0^{(A)}=\frac{\delta N_{A0}-P_0\delta D_{A0}}{D_0},
   279	\end{equation}
   280	so
   281	\begin{equation}
   282	\label{eq:app-stage023-p-anisotropy}
   283	\boxed{
   284	a_{P,0}=\frac{a_{N,0}-P_0a_{D,0}}{D_0},
   285	\qquad
   286	b_{P,0}=\frac{b_{N,0}-P_0b_{D,0}}{D_0}}.
   287	\end{equation}
   288	Thus outgoing-prefactor anisotropy can arise only from direct anisotropy in \(N_{A0}\) or conservative anisotropy in \(D_{A0}\) reweighted by \(P_0\).
   289	
   290	\stagefield{Output}{Stage~023 outputs the projectors \eqref{eq:app-stage023-projectors}, the full coupled coefficient formulas \eqref{eq:app-stage023-b-moments}, \eqref{eq:app-stage023-z-moments-port}, \eqref{eq:app-stage023-n-moments-port}, and \eqref{eq:app-stage023-d-coeffs}, the isotropic normalization ratio \eqref{eq:app-stage023-normalization-test}, the constant-prefactor conditions \eqref{eq:app-stage023-constant-prefactor-conditions}, and the anisotropy transport laws \eqref{eq:app-stage023-u-anisotropy} and \eqref{eq:app-stage023-p-anisotropy}.}

    33	def banner(title: str) -> None:
    34	    line = "=" * 88
    35	    print("\n" + line)
    36	    print(title)
    37	    print(line)
    38	
    39	
    40	def subbanner(title: str) -> None:
    41	    line = "-" * 88
    42	    print("\n" + line)
    43	    print(title)

    45	
    46	
    47	def expect_zero(name: str, expr: sp.Expr | sp.Matrix) -> None:
    48	    if isinstance(expr, sp.MatrixBase):
    49	        simplified = expr.applyfunc(lambda z: sp.simplify(sp.expand(z)))
    50	        print(f"{name} =")
    51	        sp.pprint(simplified)
    52	        if any(entry != 0 for entry in simplified):
    53	            raise AssertionError(f"{name} is not zero")
    54	    else:
    55	        simplified = sp.simplify(sp.expand(expr))
    56	        print(f"{name} = {simplified}")
    57	        if simplified != 0:
    58	            raise AssertionError(f"{name} is not zero")
    59	
    60	
    61	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62	# I. Operator-normalized -> response-normalized grouped moments
    63	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64	
    65	def operator_to_response_bridge() -> dict[str, sp.Expr]:
    66	    banner("SECTION I — CONSERVATIVE OPERATOR MOMENTS TO GROUPED RESPONSE MOMENTS")
    67	
    68	    omega = sp.symbols("omega", real=True)
    69	    D0, D2, D4 = sp.symbols("D0 D2 D4", nonzero=True, real=True)
    70	
    71	    Dcons = D0 + D2 * omega**2 + D4 * omega**4
    72	    Yresp = sp.expand(sp.series(D0 / Dcons, omega, 0, 6).removeO())
    73	    u2 = sp.simplify(Yresp.coeff(omega, 2))
    74	    u4 = sp.simplify(Yresp.coeff(omega, 4))
    75	
    76	    subbanner("I.1 — Exact normalized-response coefficients")
    77	    print("Y_resp(ω) = D0 / D_cons(ω) =")
    78	    sp.pprint(Yresp)
    79	
    80	    expect_zero("u2 formula", u2 + D2 / D0)
    81	    expect_zero("u4 formula", u4 - (D2**2 - D0 * D4) / D0**2)
    82	
    83	    print("u2 =")
    84	    sp.pprint(u2)
    85	    print("u4 =")
    86	    sp.pprint(u4)
    87	
    88	    return {"u2": u2, "u4": u4}
    89	
    90	
    91	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92	# II. Outgoing prefactor and branch coefficients
    93	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94	
    95	def outgoing_prefactor_bridge() -> dict[str, sp.Expr]:
    96	    banner("SECTION II — OUTGOING PREFACTOR AND BRANCH COEFFICIENTS")
    97	
    98	    omega = sp.symbols("omega", real=True)
    99	    D0, D2, D4 = sp.symbols("D0 D2 D4", nonzero=True, real=True)
   100	    N0, N2, N4 = sp.symbols("N0 N2 N4", real=True)
   101	    A, B, G5 = sp.symbols("A B G5", real=True)
   102	
   103	    Dcons = D0 + D2 * omega**2 + D4 * omega**4
   104	    Nfac = N0 + N2 * omega**2 + N4 * omega**4
   105	    Pref = sp.expand(sp.series(D0 * Nfac / Dcons**2, omega, 0, 6).removeO())
   106	    P0 = sp.simplify(Pref.coeff(omega, 0))
   107	    P2 = sp.simplify(Pref.coeff(omega, 2))
   108	    P4 = sp.simplify(Pref.coeff(omega, 4))
   109	
   110	    Y2_out = 1 + A * omega**2 + B * omega**4 + I * G5 * omega**5
   111	    Ybranch = sp.expand(Pref * Y2_out)
   112	    K0 = sp.simplify(Ybranch.coeff(omega, 0))
   113	    K2 = sp.simplify(Ybranch.coeff(omega, 2))
   114	    K4 = sp.simplify(Ybranch.coeff(omega, 4))
   115	    Gamma5 = sp.simplify(Ybranch.coeff(omega, 5) / I)
   116	
   117	    subbanner("II.1 — Internal prefactor coefficients")
   118	    expect_zero("P0 formula", P0 - N0 / D0)
   119	    expect_zero("P2 formula", P2 - (D0 * N2 - 2 * D2 * N0) / D0**2)
   120	    expect_zero(
   121	        "P4 formula",

   126	    sp.pprint(P0)
   127	    print("P2 =")
   128	    sp.pprint(P2)
   129	    print("P4 =")
   130	    sp.pprint(P4)
   131	
   132	    subbanner("II.2 — Full branch coefficients after multiplying by the outgoing l=2 fingerprint")
   133	    expect_zero("K0 formula", K0 - P0)
   134	    expect_zero("K2 formula", K2 - (P2 + A * P0))
   135	    expect_zero("K4 formula", K4 - (P4 + A * P2 + B * P0))
   136	    expect_zero("Gamma5 formula", Gamma5 - G5 * P0)
   137	
   138	    print("K0 =")
   139	    sp.pprint(K0)
   140	    print("K2 =")
   141	    sp.pprint(K2)
   142	    print("K4 =")
   143	    sp.pprint(K4)
   144	    print("Gamma5 =")
   145	    sp.pprint(Gamma5)
   146	
   147	    return {
   148	        "P0": P0,
   149	        "P2": P2,
   150	        "P4": P4,
   151	        "K0": K0,
   152	        "K2": K2,
   153	        "K4": K4,
   154	        "Gamma5": Gamma5,
   155	    }
   156

   158	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   159	# III. Grouped real P2 inverse formulas and isotropy defects

   161	
   162	def grouped_p2_inverse_map() -> None:
   163	    banner("SECTION III — GROUPED REAL P2 INVERSE FORMULAS AND ISOTROPY DEFECTS")
   164	
   165	    x20, x21, x22 = sp.symbols("x20 x21 x22", real=True)
   166	    xbar, ax, bx = sp.symbols("xbar ax bx", real=True)
   167	
   168	    xbar_expr = sp.simplify((x20 + 2 * x21 + 2 * x22) / 5)
   169	    ax_expr = sp.simplify((2 * x20 - x21 - x22) / 10)
   170	    bx_expr = sp.simplify((x21 - x22) / 2)
   171	
   172	    x20_back = sp.simplify(xbar + 4 * ax)
   173	    x21_back = sp.simplify(xbar - ax + bx)
   174	    x22_back = sp.simplify(xbar - ax - bx)
   175

   177	    print("xbar =", xbar_expr)
   178	    print("ax   =", ax_expr)
   179	    print("bx   =", bx_expr)
   180	
   181	    expect_zero("x20 recovered", x20_back.subs({xbar: xbar_expr, ax: ax_expr, bx: bx_expr}) - x20)
   182	    expect_zero("x21 recovered", x21_back.subs({xbar: xbar_expr, ax: ax_expr, bx: bx_expr}) - x21)
   183	    expect_zero("x22 recovered", x22_back.subs({xbar: xbar_expr, ax: ax_expr, bx: bx_expr}) - x22)
   184	
   185	    A_sq = sp.simplify(4 * ax_expr**2 + sp.Rational(4, 5) * bx_expr**2)

   193	    expect_zero("xbar_iso - xQ", xbar_iso - xQ)
   194	    expect_zero("ax_iso", ax_iso)
   195	    expect_zero("bx_iso", bx_iso)
   196

     1	# Moving-Throat PDE — Stage 022: Grouped Real `P2` Bundle and the Normalized Outgoing-Quadrupole Bridge
     2	
     3	## Purpose
     4	
     5	Stage 021 gave the matched one-lane bridge from
     6	
     7	- the conservative wall/BdG/Maxwell/mixed block,
     8	- to a passive/outgoing `l=2` fingerprint,
     9	- with a positive static transfer factor.
    10	
    11	That was enough to show **where** the 2.5PN quadrupole route can live, but not yet enough to speak the same language as the grouped real `P2` conservative packages or the 2.5PN normalization stack.
    12	
    13	The next missing bridge is therefore not “solve the whole PDE.”
    14	It is much sharper:
    15	
    16	> lift the one-lane Stage-021 result to the grouped real `20/21/22` bundle, convert the Stage-003 and Stages-004--021 conservative operator moments into the normalized grouped-response moments used by the 2.5PN package, and isolate the exact normalization product that still has to hit the universal quadrupole target.
    17	
    18	That is the whole point of this stage.
    19	
    20	---
    21	
    22	## 1. Grouped real `P2` lane setup
    23	
    24	Work lane by lane with
    25	
    26	`A in {20,21,22}`.
    27	
    28	After the conservative Stage-003 + Stages-004--021 reductions, each grouped lane is described by a conservative wall/worldtube operator
    29	
    30	`D_A^(cons)(omega) = D_{A0} + D_{A2} omega^2 + D_{A4} omega^4 + O(omega^6)`.
    31	
    32	This notation is intentionally broad.
    33	All of the following are already absorbed into the coefficients `D_{A0}, D_{A2}, D_{A4}`:
    34	
    35	- the geometry-only finite-throat wall backbone,
    36	- the stable BdG support self-energy,
    37	- the conservative localized-Maxwell / mixed-sector self-energy.
    38	
    39	So Stage 022 does **not** reopen the earlier reductions. It takes them as the conservative front end.
    40	
    41	The outgoing mixed-sector dressing from Stage 021 is encoded in a transfer factor
    42	
    43	`N_A(omega) = N_{A0} + N_{A2} omega^2 + N_{A4} omega^4 + O(omega^6)`.
    44	
    45	The key new point is that the grouped real `P2` bundle is now described by **two** low-frequency objects per lane:
    46	
    47	1. the conservative operator `D_A^(cons)`,
    48	2. the outgoing transfer factor `N_A`.
    49	
    50	---
    51	
    52	## 2. Exact bridge from conservative operator moments to grouped-response moments
    53	
    54	Stage 003 normalized the conservative operator itself.
    55	The 2.5PN grouped-quadrupole package, however, is phrased most naturally in terms of the normalized **response**
    56	
    57	`Y_A(omega) = D_{A0} / D_A^(cons)(omega)`.
    58	
    59	Expanding,
    60	
    61	`Y_A(omega) = 1 + u_2^(A) omega^2 + u_4^(A) omega^4 + O(omega^6)`.
    62	
    63	The exact conversion formulas are
    64	
    65	`u_2^(A) = - D_{A2} / D_{A0}`,
    66	
    67	`u_4^(A) = ( D_{A2}^2 - D_{A0} D_{A4} ) / D_{A0}^2`.
    68	
    69	This is the first important bookkeeping bridge of the stage.
    70	It tells us exactly how the conservative wall/BdG/Maxwell data from Stages 3–4 enter the grouped real `P2` response moments used by the 2.5PN summary.
    71	
    72	So from this point onward:
    73	
    74	- `D_{A0}, D_{A2}, D_{A4}` are the conservative operator moments,
    75	- `u_2^(A), u_4^(A)` are the normalized grouped-response moments.
    76	
    77	---
    78	
    79	## 3. Exact grouped inverse map and isotropy defects
    80	
    81	For any grouped triple `(x_20, x_21, x_22)`, define
    82	
    83	`xbar = (x_20 + 2 x_21 + 2 x_22) / 5`,
    84	
    85	`a_x = (2 x_20 - x_21 - x_22) / 10`,
    86	
    87	`b_x = (x_21 - x_22) / 2`.
    88	
    89	Then the exact inverse map is
    90	
    91	`x_20 = xbar + 4 a_x`,
    92	
    93	`x_21 = xbar - a_x + b_x`,
    94	
    95	`x_22 = xbar - a_x - b_x`.
    96	
    97	Applied to the grouped response moments, this gives
    98	
    99	`ubar_2 = (u_2^(20) + 2 u_2^(21) + 2 u_2^(22)) / 5`,
   100	
   101	`a_2 = (2 u_2^(20) - u_2^(21) - u_2^(22)) / 10`,
   102	
   103	`b_2 = (u_2^(21) - u_2^(22)) / 2`,
   104	
   105	and similarly for `(ubar_4, a_4, b_4)`.
   106	
   107	The corresponding anisotropy norm is
   108	
   109	`A_2^2 = 4 a_2^2 + (4/5) b_2^2`,
   110	
   111	and similarly for `A_4^2`.
   112	
   113	So the grouped real `P2` isotropy gate is once again completely sharp:
   114	
   115	`a_2 = b_2 = a_4 = b_4 = 0`.
   116	
   117	If the three grouped lanes share the same conservative moments, then the anisotropy defects vanish automatically.
   118	
   119	---
   120	
   121	## 4. The outgoing bridge is controlled by an exact internal prefactor
   122	
   123	To first order in the outgoing branch, the response correction is controlled by the internal prefactor
   124	
   125	`Pref_A(omega) = D_{A0} N_A(omega) / D_A^(cons)(omega)^2`.
   126	
   127	Expand
   128	
   129	`Pref_A(omega) = P_{A0} + P_{A2} omega^2 + P_{A4} omega^4 + O(omega^6)`.
   130	
   270	`mhat_0^2 * P_0 = 54 G c_s^5 / (5 a^5 c^5)`.
   271	
   272	This is the sharpest expression of the remaining theorem gap I can give at this stage.
   273	
   274	Everything that came before was needed to make this quantity well defined.
   275	Now the problem is no longer diffuse:
   276	
   277	> compute the isotropic moving-throat value of the invariant product `mhat_0^2 P_0`.
   278	
   279	On the natural source-map branch `mhat_0 = 1 + O(a^2/r^2)`, this reduces to the familiar 2.5PN target
   280	
   281	`P_0 = 54 G c_s^5 / (5 a^5 c^5)`
   282	
   283	at leading point-particle order.
   284	
   285	If, in addition, the outgoing branch is carried by a **constant isotropic prefactor** (`P_2 = P_4 = 0`), then the even branch coefficients automatically become
   286	
   287	`K_2 = P_0 a^2 / (9 c_s^2)`,
   288	
   289	`K_4 = 4 P_0 a^4 / (81 c_s^4)`.
   290	
   291	That is exactly the constant-prefactor branch isolated in the 2.5PN normalization package.
   292	
   293	---
   294	
   295	## 9. What this stage accomplishes physically
   296	
   297	This stage does three things that were missing before.
   298	
   299	### 9.1 It unifies the Stage-003 and Stages-004--021 conservative data with the 2.5PN grouped language
   300	
   301	Before this note, the conservative moving-throat derivation was naturally phrased in terms of operator moments.
   302	The 2.5PN grouped summary, however, is phrased in terms of normalized grouped-response coefficients and branch-normalization data.
   303	
   304	Stage 022 now gives the exact conversion formulas between those languages.
   305	
   306	### 9.2 It identifies the true internal quantity that controls 2.5PN
   307	
   308	The leading odd coefficient does **not** depend on every conservative detail equally.
   309	At the universal point-particle 2.5PN level, the only internal quantity that matters is the isotropic static outgoing prefactor `P_0 = N_0 / D_0` (or `mhat_0^2 P_0` in invariant form).
   310	
   311	That is a much sharper theorem target than “solve the whole quadrupole branch.”
   312	
   313	### 9.3 It explains exactly what the full PDE still has to provide
   314	
   315	The full moving-throat PDE still has to do two hard things:
   316	
   317	1. produce the true grouped-lane conservative coefficients `D_{A0}, D_{A2}, D_{A4}` and outgoing transfer coefficients `N_{A0}, N_{A2}, N_{A4}` on the coupled isotropic branch,
   318	2. and land the invariant product `mhat_0^2 P_0` on the target value above.
   319	
   320	That is now a concrete job description for the missing PDE.
   321	
   322	---
   323	
   324	## 10. Immediate next step

   327	
   328	Do **not** jump yet to the full nonlinear theorem.
   329	Instead:

   333	3. evaluate the corresponding `P_0, P_2, P_4` prefactor data on the isotropic branch,
   334	4. and test whether the invariant normalization product `mhat_0^2 P_0` lands on the universal target.
   335	
   336	That is the smallest next theorem gate that directly attacks the remaining 2.5PN/4PN bottleneck.

   200	\paragraph{5. First-order normalization transport.}
   201	If
   202	\begin{equation}
   203	D_A=D_0+\varepsilon\lambda_A D_1+O(\varepsilon^2),
   204	\qquad
   205	N_A=N_0+\varepsilon\lambda_A N_1+O(\varepsilon^2),
   206	\end{equation}
   207	then
   208	\begin{equation}
   209	\label{eq:app-stage024-PA-transport}
   210	P_A=\frac{N_A}{D_A}=P_0+\varepsilon\lambda_A P_1+O(\varepsilon^2),
   211	\qquad
   212	\boxed{P_1=\frac{N_1D_0-N_0D_1}{D_0^2}}.
   213	\end{equation}

     2	"""
     3	Projected Maxwell → mouth-Taylor → 5PN gate bridge.

     8	    X_proj(ell) = X_0 + ell * mu1 * X_0' + O(ell^2)
     9	
    10	for the primitive one-port packet X in {Q, S2, H_port, Delta, P, G_W}.

    22	
    23	
    24	def assert_zero(label, expr):
    25	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    26	    if residue != 0:
    27	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} failed: {sp.sstr(residue)}")
    28	
    29	
    30	def assert_nonzero(label, expr):
    31	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    32	    if residue == 0:
    33	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} unexpectedly vanished")
    34	
    35	
    36	# Base one-port variables

    50	z4 = (-Delta**2*Hport*s1 - Delta**2*S2*h1 - Delta**2*q1 + 2*Delta*Hport*S2*d1 + 2*Delta*Q*S2*s1 + 2*Delta*Q*d1 + Delta*S2**2*q1 - 3*Q*S2**2*d1)/Delta**4

    55

    99	# Real derivative-map content lives in z2d, z4d, n2d, n4d, where multiple
   100	# primitive slips cross-contract. Anchor those four to a structural property
   101	# that requires the Taylor lift to be linear in each primitive slip:
   102	for _slip in (Qx, Sx, Hx, Dx, Px, Gx):
   103	    for _name, _expr in (("z2d", z2d), ("z4d", z4d), ("n2d", n2d), ("n4d", n4d)):
   104	        assert_zero(f"{_name} is linear in {_slip}", sp.diff(_expr, _slip, 2))
   105	
   106	# NOTE: The next five independence checks are construction-level, not physical:
   107	# Xi (line 67), K1 (line 68), H_even (line 69) are *defined* without any
   108	# dependence on the listed primitive slips. To exercise the factorization
   109	# claim non-trivially, we below also re-derive the bottlenecks from a
   110	# naive bundle pull-back that contains all six slips, and assert that the
   111	# cancellation to the three-/four-slip form is an algebraic identity.
   112	for sym in (Sx, Hx, Gx):
   113	    assert_zero(f"Xi_load independence from {sym}", sp.diff(Xi, sym))
   114	for sym in (Px, Gx):
   115	    assert_zero(f"K1 independence from {sym}", sp.diff(K1, sym))
   116	    assert_zero(f"H_even independence from {sym}", sp.diff(He, sym))
   117	# Bundle pull-back consistency: rebuild Xi, K1, H_even from z0d/z2d/z4d/n0d
   118	# (which DO contain all six primed slips before simplification), and assert
   119	# the rebuilt forms equal the directly-substituted Xi, K1, H_even.
   120	Xi_bundle = sp.simplify(2*Px/P - 2*Dx/Delta + Qx/(D0*Delta) - Q*Dx/(D0*Delta**2))
   121	assert_zero("Xi bundle pull-back consistency", Xi - Xi_bundle)
   122	K1_bundle = sp.simplify(-(z2d + z0d/sp.Integer(9)))
   123	assert_zero("K1 bundle pull-back consistency", K1 - K1_bundle)
   124	He_bundle = sp.simplify(-(z4d) + sp.Rational(2,3)*z2d - z0d/sp.Integer(27))
   125	assert_zero("H_even bundle pull-back consistency", He - He_bundle)
   126	assert_zero("d Xi_load / d Pprime", coef_Xi_Px - 2/P)
   127	assert_zero("d deltaP2 / d G_W prime", coef_dP2_Gx + 2*P/(D0*Delta**2))
   128	assert_nonzero("deltaP4 should depend on G_W prime", coef_dP4_Gx)
   129	assert_nonzero("source/denominator sieve determinant", qd_matrix.det())
   130	assert_nonzero("spectral sieve determinant", sh_matrix.det())
   131	assert_zero("compensation Hport denominator", Hx_den - 9*Delta**2*(Delta*Hport - Q*S2))
   132	assert_zero("compensation S2 denominator", Sx_den - 9*Delta*(Delta*Hport - Q*S2))
   133	assert_nonzero("mutated compensation denominator should fail", Hx_den - 9*Delta**2*(Delta*Hport + Q*S2))
   134	
   135	if qd_only != [{Qx: 0, Dx: 0}]:
   136	    raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected pure source/denominator solve: {qd_only}")
   137	if sh_only != [{Sx: 0, Hx: 0}]:
   138	    raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected pure spectral solve: {sh_only}")
   139	# Note: the next two assertions are tautological by sp.solve's contract
   140	# (comp_surface is defined as the solve output), kept here for visual
   141	# symmetry with the explicit denominator factorization assertions above.
   142	assert_zero("compensation K1", K1.subs(comp_surface))
   143	assert_zero("compensation H_even", He.subs(comp_surface))
   144	Z2_slot = (Q*S2 - Hport*Delta)/Delta**2
   145	assert_zero("compensation Hport denominator equals -9 Delta^4 Z2", Hx_den - (-9*Delta**4*Z2_slot))
   146	assert_zero("compensation S2 denominator equals -9 Delta^3 Z2", Sx_den - (-9*Delta**3*Z2_slot))
   147	
   148	def p(expr):
   149	    return sp.factor(sp.simplify(expr))
   150	
   151	print("="*96)
   152	print("1) Mouth-local Taylor ansatz")
   153	print("="*96)
   154	print("Take a normalized one-sided mouth kernel w(u), u >= 0, with first moment mu1 = ∫ u w(u) du.")
   155	print("Then for any primitive one-port quantity X(s),")
   156	print("  X_proj(ell) = X(0) + ell * mu1 * X'(0) + O(ell^2).")
   157	print("So")
   158	print("  q1 = mu1*Q'(0),   s1 = mu1*S2'(0),   h1 = mu1*H_port'(0),")
   159	print("  d1 = mu1*Delta'(0), p1 = mu1*P'(0),  g1 = mu1*G_W'(0).")
   160	print("For a symmetric interior kernel, mu1 = 0 and the whole first layer vanishes.\n")
   161	
   162	print("="*96)
   163	print("2) Primitive-to-bundle mouth-derivative map (divide out mu1)")
   164	print("="*96)
   165	print("z0/mu1 =")
   166	print(" ", p(z0d))
   167	print("z2/mu1 =")
   168	print(" ", p(z2d))
   169	print("z4/mu1 =")
   170	print(" ", p(z4d))
   171	print("n0/mu1 =")
   172	print(" ", p(n0d))
   173	print("n2/mu1 =")
   174	print(" ", p(n2d))
   175	print("n4/mu1 =")
   176	print(" ", p(n4d))
   177	print()
   178	
   179	print("="*96)
   180	print("3) Direct projected-Maxwell contribution to the 5PN bottlenecks")
   181	print("="*96)
   182	print("Xi_load^(proj)/mu1 =")
   183	print(" ", p(Xi))
   184	print("K1^(proj)/mu1 =")
   185	print(" ", p(K1))
   186	print("H_even^(proj)/mu1 =")
   187	print(" ", p(He))
   188	print()
   189	print("Immediate dependency pattern:")
   190	print("  - Xi_load depends only on (Q', Delta', P').")
   191	print("  - K1 and H_even depend only on (Q', Delta', S2', H_port').")
   192	print("  - G_W' does not enter Xi_load, K1, or H_even.")
   193	print("  - G_W' first enters the constant-prefactor transport delta P2, delta P4.\n")
   194	
   195	print("="*96)
   196	print("4) Isotropic compatibility and constant-prefactor transport")
   197	print("="*96)
   198	print("delta Compat / mu1 =")
   199	print(" ", p(Compat))
   200	print("delta P2 / mu1 =")
   201	print(" ", p(deltaP2))
   202	print("delta P4 / mu1 =")
   203	print(" ", p(deltaP4))
   204	print()
   205	print("Useful direct coefficients:")
   206	print("  d(Xi_load)/dP'  =")
   207	print("   ", p(coef_Xi_Px))
   208	print("  d(delta P2)/dG_W' =")
   209	print("   ", p(coef_dP2_Gx))
   210	print("  d(delta P4)/dG_W' =")
   211	print("   ", p(coef_dP4_Gx))
   212	print()
   213	
   214	print("="*96)
   215	print("5) Mechanism sieve")
   216	print("="*96)
   217	print("Case A: source/denominator sector only, S2' = H_port' = 0")
   218	print("  solve K1 = H_even = 0  ->")
   219	print("  ", qd_only)
   220	print("Case B: spectral sector only, Q' = Delta' = 0")
   221	print("  solve K1 = H_even = 0  ->")
   222	print("  ", sh_only)
   223	print()
   224	print("So neither a pure (Q', Delta') correction nor a pure (S2', H_port') correction")
   225	print("can close the even gates nontrivially.")
   226	print()
   227	
   228	print("="*96)
   229	print("6) Exact mixed compensation surface for the even gates")
   230	print("="*96)
   231	print("Solving K1 = H_even = 0 for (H_port', S2') gives")
   232	print("H_port' =")
   233	print(" ", p(comp_surface[Hx]))
   234	print("S2' =")
   235	print(" ", p(comp_surface[Sx]))
   236	print()
   237	print("The compensation denominator is proportional to")
   238	print("  Delta*H_port - Q*S2.")
   239	print("This is exactly the primitive combination behind the conservative Z2 slot:")
   240	print("  Z2 = (Q*S2 - H_port*Delta)/Delta^2.")
   241	print("So the even-gate compensation becomes singular on the surface Delta*H_port = Q*S2")
   242	print("(equivalently Z2 = 0).")
   243	print()
   244	
   245	print("="*96)
   246	print("7) Readout")
   247	print("="*96)
   248	print("The mouth-local projected-Maxwell packet factorizes into three jobs:")
   249	print("  (i)  (Q', Delta', S2', H_port') repair the conservative even gates K1 and H_even,")
   250	print("  (ii) P' directly tunes the weak-axisymmetric prefactor slope Xi_load,")
   251	print("  (iii) G_W' first tunes the constant-prefactor transport through delta P2 and delta P4.")
   252	print()
   253	print("This is precisely the kind of multi-channel structure you would expect from a real")
   254	print("near-throat missing piece, and it is much sharper than a single scalar correction.")
   255	print("STATUS: PASS")
